HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B001","root_000266/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_037d4628eeb8daba43e7","text":"2. **Garden-cover functions as selective protection.** **Lexical:** `ج ن ن` B001, covering and concealment, and B008, protective cover. **Cue:** the garden encloses a realm where no `لَٰغِيَة` is heard in 88:11 and where water, rest, and drink are deliberately arranged in 88:12-15. **Role:** the cover filters harmful acoustic and material exposure while retaining beneficial provision. **Abduction:** I infer that the enclosure enables the quiet and security; the text gives co-location, not an architectural causal statement. **Reading change:** `جنة` reads not only as vegetation but as a cover whose selectivity helps constitute a sheltered, provisioned habitat.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B001","root_000266/B003","root_000266/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_069dde56627530f49d3f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:10:2\",\"root_000266\",[\"B001\",\"B003\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bahçe, kuşatıcı felaketin karşısında koruyucu ve seçici bir örtüdür; zararlı sesi dışarıda bırakırken suyu, dinlenmeyi ve hizmeti içeride tutar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B003","root_000266/B011"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_16d0d474a2a273302440","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:10:2\",\"root_000266\",[\"B003\",\"B011\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Gür büyüme çağrışımı ve ardından gelen kaynak, bahçeyi yalnız varılacak bir yer değil, suyla sürdürülen canlı bir ekoloji olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001042/B002","root_001281/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3c94a0da2e53f23f8937","text":"3. **High rank rather than only altitude.** Evidence: `ع ل و` B002 nobility/high rank, B003 haughty domination, B004 mastery; later `ك ب ر` B005/B006 rank/majesty and `س طر` B003 control negated for the messenger. Structural cue: height vocabulary culminates in the high garden, raised sky, and greater punishment. Role: `عالية` marks honorable elevation, but the surrounding account prevents it from becoming arrogant domination. Abductive move: legitimate elevation is granted placement, not self-magnification. Reading change: the garden’s height is moral/status elevation as much as vertical altitude.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001042/B001","root_001042/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_4c773ce3247ad0c72332","text":"3. **Height is calibrated rank, not remoteness.** **Lexical:** `ع ل و` B001, rising height, and B002, nobility and high rank. **Cue:** `عَالِيَةٍ` is echoed by raised couches in 88:13, yet cups are deliberately set down in 88:14 and cushions aligned in 88:15. **Role:** physical elevation marks honor, while lower placement makes provision accessible inside that honor. **Abduction:** I infer deliberate vertical calibration from the alternation of raised and placed objects. **Reading change:** the garden's height is not inaccessible distance; it is an ordered dignity whose resources are brought within use.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000266/B003","root_000266/B011","root_001042/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5c6c510aa48bb2b81ed1","text":"1. **The garden is a water-sustained, actively growing enclosure.** **Lexical:** `ج ن ن` B003, \"tree-covered garden,\" and B011, \"thick or vigorous growth\"; `ع ل و` B001, physical height. **Cue:** the locative declaration is followed by a running spring in 88:12 and by furnishings whose raised, placed, and rowed states occupy the enclosure in 88:13-15. **Role:** garden-cover defines the habitat, vigorous growth makes it living rather than scenic, and the spring supplies its inferred sustaining flow. **Abduction:** I infer irrigation from proximity; the packet never states that the spring waters the garden. **Reading change:** the ayah describes an elevated ecology in operation, not a bare destination label.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001042/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a6ea530a4081d43c403a","text":"1. **Covered garden raised above the low faces.** Evidence: `ج نن` B003 tree-covered garden, B001 concealment/covering; `ع ل و` B001 rising/high; earlier `خ ش ع` B002 low ground. Structural cue: the garden follows satisfied endeavor and contrasts with humbled low faces. Role: garden supplies protective/enclosed cover; highness supplies vertical reversal. Abductive move: not every covering is punitive; the same broad cover-imagery can be sheltering when joined to favor. Reading change: the garden reads as elevated protective enclosure, not merely a location.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ab100b91ac6bc8c691f9","text":"88:11 activated the protective rather than merely concealing potential of `ج ن ن` by making the enclosure acoustically selective. 88:12-15 then corrected height from remoteness into calibrated access: growth is supplied, couches are raised, cups are set down, and cushions are aligned.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001042/B001","root_001042/B002","root_001042/B003","root_001042/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cb0c611edb80bfe4b9b0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:10:3\",\"root_001042\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yükseklik hem mekânı hem de onurlu konumu anlatır; fakat aşağıya konmuş erişilebilir eşyalar ve sonraki denetim sınırı, bu yüksekliği uzaklık ya da zorba üstünlük olmaktan çıkarır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dd6b013301fd9682f4d7","text":"The later sky raised and mountains set made `عالية` participate in a cosmic architecture. The negation of messenger control and the divine greater punishment separated legitimate elevation from domination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_e29ec2323ce3c2e8d66b","text":"2. **Hidden interior against the public face.** Evidence: `ج نن` B010 hidden heart/inner matter; `و ج ه` B001 outward face in 88:8; `ر ض و` B001 inward acceptance. Structural cue: face, satisfaction, and garden appear in sequence. Role: the garden externalizes an inward state of hidden contentment.
